(National UFO Reporting Center, 'NUFORC UFO Sighting 182416,' August 22, 2024) 2024, July 29 - The American Institute of Aeronautics and Astronautics conference held in Las Vegas, Nevada, hosts three sessions on UAP, one of them, 'The Reported Shape, Size, Kinematics, Electromagnetic Effects, and Presence of Sound of Unidentified Aerial Phenomena from Select Reports, 1947-2016,' led by researcher Robert Powell. (Keith Basterfield, 'UAP Sessions Held at the 2024 American Institute of Aeronautics and Astronautics Conference,' Unidentified Anomalous Phenomena-Scientific Research, August 3, 2024) Intelligence Authorization Act that includes an amendment with revised UAP-related language. This new version

2024, July 31 - Sens. Mark Warner (D-Va.) and Marco Rubio (R-Fla.), respectively the chairman and ranking Republican on the Senate Select Committee on Intelligence, file a revised version of the Fiscal Year 2025 expands the list of congressional committees that must be notified in order to fund any Intelligence Community special access program. (Douglas Dean Johnson, 'Congressional UAP/UAP Update: Revised Intelligence Authorization Act,' X posting, August 1, 2024)

- 2024, August 1 - 3:40 p.m. Near Fort Edward, New York, a pilot and co-pilot witness a bright green, cigar-shaped UFO that moves rapidly 200-300 feet above their aircraft and has no wings. The pilot estimates that it is about the size of a Boeing 747. After the sighting, both the pilot and co-pilot are questioned by a mysterious individual, and the co-pilot is grounded the following day. (National UFO Reporting Center, 'NUFORC UFO Sighting 182552,' August 22, 2024)
- 2024, August 6 - The Brazilian Air Force releases a new set of UAP documents for 2023. The files contain 35 'hotel traffic records' ( ocorrência de tráfego hotel ), the name given by the Brazilian Air Force to UFOs encountered by pilots during flight. (Rony Vernet, 'Brazilian National Archives Released New Batch of UAP Documents,' UAP Check, August 7, 2024)
- 2024, August 14 - Due to the multiple drone incursions (reported as 'a bright light, a hovercraft-like aircraft, and unusual flight patterns, including abrupt stops and zigzag movements') in previous months, the Federal Aviation Administration imposes temporary flight restrictions on 'uncrewed aerial systems' around Palmdale (Calif.) Airport, which shares its runways with the US Air Force's Plant 42, home to Lockheed Martin's Skunk Works advanced projects division. The restrictions are scheduled to be lifted on September 15. (Joseph Trevithick and Howard Altman, 'Palmdale UFO Scare Leads to Revelations about Mystery Drone Incursions over Secretive Plant 42,' The War Zone, August 19, 2024; 'Multiple UFO Sightings in California: What We Know,' Newsweek, August 19, 2024)
- 2024, August 16 - 10:00 p.m. Two individuals are night fishing out of the Buffalo Small Boat Harbor in Buffalo, New York, when they see two triangular objects hovering below the clouds over the Canadian side of Lake Erie. Initially, the witnesses notice red lights surrounding a white light, which pulsates while tracing the perimeter of the closest object. The nearer object makes some 'crazy, jarring, geometric moves,' demonstrating its ability to accelerate instantaneously. After these movements, the more distant object takes off towards Canada, while the closer object crosses Lake Erie into the US. Both eventually disappear from sight. (National UFO Reporting Center, 'NUFORC UFO Sighting 182661,' August 22, 2024)
- 2024, August 16-19 - The Society for UAP Studies hosts an online conference that addresses how professionals in the humanities, social sciences, and natural sciences can help advance our understanding of UFOs. (Micah Hanks, 'Academics Unite to Bring Humanities to the Study of Aerial Mysteries at Society for UAP Studies Event,' The Debrief, August 1, 2024; Baptiste Friscourt, 'The Birth of a New Science: Academic UAP Research,' Substack: Sentinel News, August 17, 2024; Bryan Sentes, 'Varieties and Trajectories of Contemporary UAP Studies: Reflections on the Most Recent Society for UAP Studies' Annual Conference,' Skunkworks, September 1, 2024)
- 2024, August 20 - HarperCollins publishes Luis Elizondo's memoir, Imminent: Inside the Pentagon's Hunt for UFOs, about his time with the Pentagon's Advanced Aerospace Threat Identification Program. (Ralph Blumenthal and Leslie Kean, 'A Memoir Offers an Insider's Perspective into the Pentagon's U.F.O. Hunt,' New York Times, August 16, 2024; Christopher Sharp, 'Imminent Disclosure: Lue Elizondo's Revelations Poised to Shake UFO Discourse and Pressure US Government,' Liberation Times, August 20, 2024; Keith Basterfield, 'What's New in Elizondo's Book,' Unidentified Anomalous Phenomena-Scientific Research, September 15, 2024)
- 2024, August 22 - 9:48 p.m. Two individuals driving on South Mill Creek Road near Salem, Indiana, see a black, triangular-shaped object hovering directly above their car. It shoots an intense blue and white beam down on them from 100 feet away. The object, in shape similar to a stealth bomber, hovers briefly before speeding away. Afterward, one of the witnesses is informed that more than 80 people have reported seeing something in the sky around the same time. (National UFO Reporting Center, 'NUFORC UFO Sighting 182743,' August 23, 2024)
- 2024, August 26 - AARO announces that Jon T. Kosloski has been selected as its new full-time director. Kosloski has held positions within the Research Directorate of the National Security Agency and served as a subject matter expert in the area of Free Space Optics. (Micah Hanks, 'The Pentagon's Official UAP Investigative Office Has a New Full-Time Director,' The Debrief, August 26, 2024; Kevin D. Randle, 'Dr. Jon T. Kosloski Takes over AARO and It's More of the Same,' A Different Perspective, August 31, 2024)
- 2024, September 18 - 7:30 p.m. A witness is driving northbound on US Highway 199 south of Smithfield, Pennsylvania, when three balls of light suddenly appear on her left and move across the road in front of her at hood level. The objects move in a straight line toward a field on the right side of the road with only a few feet between them. Each sphere is white and about one foot in diameter. They disappear after a couple of seconds. (Stan Gordon, 'Three Low Level Glowing Spheres Nearly Strike Car near Smithfield, Pennsylvania, September 18, 2024,' Stan Gordon's UFO Anomalies Zone, September 24, 2024; Stan Gordon, 'Update on the Smithfield, PA. UFO/UAP Stalled Vehicle Incident,' Stan Gordon's UFO Anomalies Zone, October 1, 2024)
- 2024, September 19 - Senate Armed Services Committee Chairman Sen. Jack Reed (D-R.I.) and ranking Republican Roger Wicker (R-Miss.) file a massive 'manager's package' (SA 3290) to the National Defense Authorization

Act (S. 4638). However, it does not include the text of the Rounds-Schumer UAP Disclosure Act (SA 2610). ('UFO Update: The UAP Disclosure Act Fails to Make the Cut for the Manager's Amendment to the National Defense Authorization Act,' D. Dean Johnson, X post, September 20, 2024; Christopher Sharp, 'ParadigmChanging UFO Transparency Legislation Fails in Congress for Second Consecutive Year,' Liberation Times, September 20, 2024; 'Mystery, Confusion, and Allegations Surround the Downfall of Key UFO Legislation as Glimmer of Hope Emerges,' Liberation Times, October 1, 2024)

- 2024, September 19 - The Pentagon's Defense Innovation Unit announces a collaboration with Singapore's Ministry of Defence to develop new technologies and defense systems to detect and disrupt unauthorized drone (UAS) incursions. (US Defense Innovation Unit, 'U.S.-Singapore Joint Challenge Launched to Identify Resilient Communications and Counter-Drone Technology,' September 18, 2024; Micah Hanks, 'Concerns over Unauthorized UAS Incidents Prompt New International Defense Collaboration,' The Debrief, September 23, 2024)
- 2024, September 23 - After three years, the Federal Aviation Administration declines to release emails and other documents regarding its input to the UAP Task Force on the grounds of national security. (John Greenewald, 'FAA Cites National Security to Withhold UAP Communications Despite Unclassified Report,' The Black Vault, September 23, 2024)
- 2024, September 26 - The National Oceanic and Atmospheric Administration (NOAA) releases some UAP-related correspondence with AARO and the UAP Task Force in response to a FOIA request. One of the documents includes an invitation for AARO personnel to visit NOAA's Sterling Field Support Center. This visit is part of broader interagency efforts to coordinate on the complexities of UAP sightings. Other correspondence revealed NOAA's meetings with Navy officials during the days of the UAP Task Force, highlighting the agency's longstanding role in supporting UAP investigations. (John Greenewald, 'NOAA Releases UAP-Related Correspondence,' The Black Vault, September 26, 2024)
- 2024, September 28 - 8:00 p.m. An astrophotographer and his wife are stargazing on their backyard deck in Spearfish, South Dakota, when they notice a massive, chevron-shaped craft with a grayish aura and structured white lights, crossing the sky in just 10 seconds. The object appears to be in the upper atmosphere or low Earth orbit, moving silently at an estimated speed of 523,440 mph-around 238 times faster than the Lockheed SR-71 Blackbird. The couple initially sees a hazy orb, followed by the large craft gliding smoothly from northwest to east, resembling a ghostly, ethereal 'stingray.' The astrophotographer knows of no comparable man-made object that can fly that fast. Both draw matching depictions of the object, which they later recreate digitally. (National UFO Reporting Center, 'NUFORC UFO Sighting 183257,' October 3, 2024)
- 2024, October 5 - The Dutch Safety Board (Onderzoeksraad voor Veiligheid) announces that it will accept reports from Dutch aviation professionals who have encountered UAP. The board will then decide whether an investigation is warranted. (Joachim Dekkers, 'Dutch Safety Board Accepts UAP Reports,' UAP Check, October 5, 2024) 2024, October 8 - Journalist and activist Michael Shellenberger reveals on his subscription news site, Public, that he has contacted a new, unnamed whistleblower who alleges that the executive branch of the US government has been running since 2017 a highly classified and unacknowledged Special Access Program, called Immaculate Constellation, to collect, analyze, and quarantine instrumented UAP observations by the military and intelligence services. It involves a database of videos and images taken from infrared (IR), forward-looking infrared (FLIR), full-motion video (FMV), and still photos of UAP. The program also apparently involves the retrieval of exotic technologies from which 'Alien Reproduction Vehicles' might be constructed. Another unnamed source states that this program is under the control of the Office of the Undersecretary of Defense and Intelligence. On October 9, Department of Defense spokesperson Sue Gough denies that the Pentagon has such a program. (Michael Shellenberger, 'Pentagon Is Illegally Hiding Secret UFO Program from Congress, Whistleblowers Allege,' Public, October 8, 2024; Baptiste Friscourt, 'New Whistleblower Claims Knowledge of Secret US UFO Program,' Sentinel News, October 8, 2024; Steph Whiteside, 'What Is the Pentagon's Alleged 'Immaculate Constellation' Program?' NewsNation, October 9, 2024; 'Pentagon Denies Report Claiming to Reveal Name of Top-Secret UFO Program 'for the First Time,'' New York Post, October 9, 2024; Tim McMillan, 'Opinion: Immaculate Constellation? Could the Government Really Hide a Secret UFO Program?' The Debrief, October 10, 2024; Kevin D. Randle, 'Immaculate Constellation: My Analysis,' A Different Perspective, October 11, 2024)
- 2024, October 10 - The US National Archives and Records Administration extends by one year the deadline for federal agencies to transfer their digital copies of all publicly disclosable records relating to UAP to the national archives. The new deadline is September 30, 2025. The directive was signed into law on December 22, 2023, through the

2024 National Defense Authorization Act. (William Fischer and Chris Naylor, 'AC 04.2025,' NARA Federal Records Management, October 10, 2024)

2024, October 16 - Sen. Jerry Moran (R-Kan.) prepares a bill 'to provide the Department of Defense with the necessary authorities to engage drones or unidentified aircrafts that breech our military airspace before it is too late to respond,' such as occurred at Langley AFB in December 2023. ('Sen. Moran to Introduce Legislation after Reports of Unidentified Aircrafts Hovering over U.S. Military Base,' Sen. Jerry Moran, October 16, 2024)

- 2024, October 21 - 11:00 p.m. A military-trained observer is camping alone in the Santa Rosa Mountain Yellow Post campsites near Ribbonwood, California, when suddenly all natural animal and insect noises cease and the entire area lights up brighter than the full moon. When he looks out from the bed of his truck, he sees a bright white oval light, about 20-30 feet in length, hovering silently at treetop level around 300 feet away. The light illuminates the entire area and remains completely still and noiseless, resembling a large helicopter in size but emitting no heat. After observing it for one minute, the witness retreats back to his truck bed. He falls asleep and awakens at 2:30 a.m., drenched in sweat, with no trace of the light remaining. (National UFO Reporting Center, 'NUFORC UFO Sighting 183560,' November 8, 2024)
- 2024, October 22 - The National UFO Historical Records Center holds a ribbon-cutting for its new facility, provided by the Rio Rancho (N.Mex.) Public Schools. Executive Director David Marler said, 'It's a traditional historical archive dedicated to a non-traditional subject. We want serious research to be conducted here in the hopes ultimately of maybe gaining a better insight into this mystery that we've been dealing with now for 75 plus years.' ('Public UFO Records Archive Opens in Rio Rancho,' KOB-TV, Albuquerque, New Mexico, October 22, 2024)
- 2024, October 26 - 7:45 a.m. A witness in Fort Collins, Colorado, sees a silent, dark metallic-gray, triangular object about 30 feet on each side, flying directly overhead in an apartment parking lot. The craft, featuring white lights at each corner and a light in the middle of its left side, moves southward from 90° to 60° overhead in approximately 5 seconds, disappearing from view. Its silent, steady movement and distinct shape differs from any familiar aircraft or drones the witness has previously seen in the area. (National UFO Reporting Center, 'NUFORC UFO Sighting 183508,' November 8, 2024)
- 2024, November 1 - 9:20 p.m. A commercial aircraft that has just taken off from Salt Lake City (Utah) International Airport en route to Rogue Valley International-Medford Airport in Oregon levels off at 36,000 feet and notices a group of about 5 or 6 bright white lights circling one another. The lights increase in speed corresponding to the intensity of the light. At one point, they form a triangle and remain motionless. The pilot and crew watch them for 45-50 minutes. (National UFO Reporting Center, 'NUFORC UFO Sighting 183840,' November 8, 2024)
- 2024, November 2 - Night. The same crew of the commercial aircraft that reported lights on November 1 watches a remarkable display on a flight from Los Angeles, California, to Boise, Idaho. A series of bright white lights form triangular shapes, move at high speeds, and exhibit unusual behaviors. Initially, two lights appear, followed by a vivid triangular formation of three lights that seems to rotate and shift. Additional lights join up with them, creating multiple triangles, some of which grow in size or appear to collapse into lines. The lights continue throughout the flight, often forming groups of five, and seem to center over northern California and central Oregon. During descent, the pilot observes two glowing lights in the clouds, which then disappear. (National UFO Reporting Center, 'NUFORC UFO Sighting 183844,' November 8, 2024)
- 2024, November 12 - Rep Tim Burchett (R-Tenn.) introduces the UAP Whistleblower Protection Act that would provide protections to whistleblowers who bring attention to federal funding that is being used to study UAPs. ('Rep. Burchett Introduces Bill Protecting UAP Whistleblowers,' Congressman Tim Burchett, November 12, 2024)
- 2024, November 13 - The US Navy formally denies access to 78 'documents' containing photos marked as 'unidentified aerial [anomalous] phenomena' (UAP). The denial highlights the Navy's continued use of national defense and intelligence exemptions to keep UAP data classified. The response arrives less than an hour before a highly anticipated UAP hearing on Capitol Hill, where members of Congress hear testimony about the government's lack of transparency on the issue. (John Greenewald, 'Navy Blocks Release of UAP Photos Amid Capitol Hill Hearing on Government Secrecy,' The Black Vault, November 14, 2024)
- 2024, November 13 - The US House Committee on Oversight and Accountability holds a joint subcommittee hearing on 'Unidentified Anomalous Phenomena: Exposing the Truth.' The witnesses include retired Navy Rear Adm. Timothy Gallaudet, Luis Elizondo, former NASA Associate Administrator Michael Gold, and journalist Michael Shellenberger. In her opening remarks, Subcommittee on Cybersecurity, Information Technology, and Government Innovation Chair Rep. Nancy Mace (R-S.C.) references an anonymous, untitled 12-page document provided by Shellenberger that discusses the unacknowledged Immaculate Constellation SAP, as well as government imagery, raw data collected by military personnel, restricted historical records, and signals

intelligence related to UAP. Gallaudet refers to his personal experience coming to learn of a now-famous US Navy video, popularly known as 'Go Fast,' and how an email he and others received from another U.S. Navy official disappeared following the incident. Elizondo states that 'UAP are real. Advanced technologies not made by our government-or any other government-are monitoring sensitive military installations around the globe.' Shellenberger discusses UAP whistleblower claims he learned of from sources who spoke with him on background, some of whom have asserted the US Executive Branch has managed UAP programs and related issues for decades without congressional oversight. Gold acknowledges the stigmas that have long surrounded the topic, emphasizing that such attitudes even complicated his colleagues' efforts with NASA's UAP Independent Study Team. ('Unidentified Anomalous Phenomena: Exposing the Truth,' GOP Oversight YouTube channel, November 13, 2024; Bill Chappell, 'Experts Testify Before Lawmakers That the U.S. Is Running Secret UAP Programs,' National Public Radio, November 13, 2024; Micah Hanks, 'Former Officials Warn Lawmakers of Alleged Secret UAP Programs Operating beyond Congressional Oversight,' The Debrief, November 13, 2024; Kevin D. Randle, 'Committee on Oversight and Accountability Meeting November 13,' A Different Perspective, November 13, 2024)

2024, November 14 - AARO releases its 2024 annual report on UAP, covering incidents reported from May 2023 through June 2024. According to the report, AARO received a total of 757 UAP reports during this period. These new additions bring the total number of UAP cases reviewed by AARO to over 1,600 as of June 1, 2024. The majority of the reports describe orbs, lights, and cylinders, but about 4% fall into the category of 'other' and include unique descriptions like 'green fireball,' 'a jelly fish with [multicolored] flashing lights,' or a 'silver rocket approximately six feet long.' Only 21 cases are designated as requiring further analysis to determine a cause. The report also offers some details on how the Pentagon's GREMLIN deployable sensors, developed by Georgia Tech Research Institute to gather data about UAP incidents, actually work. (All-Domain Anomaly Resolution Office, Fiscal Year 2024 Consolidated Annual Report on Unidentified Anomalous Phenomena, November 2024; John Greenewald, 'DoD Releases 2024 UAP Annual Report: 757 New Sightings Recorded Amid Growing National Security Concerns,' The Black Vault, November 14, 2024; 'Annual UFO Report Finds 21 Cases of More than 700 Received Need More Analysis,' CBS News, November 14, 2024; Howard Altman, 'Military's UFO-Hunting Aerial Surveillance System Detailed in Report,' The War Zone, November 14, 2024)
